- **medical-insurance-product-research**: 医疗险产品研究：按（可模糊的）产品名称检索、下载并归档条款、费率表、健康告知、责任免除/既往症清单、投保须知、医院与药品清单等全部资料；按统一字段抽取并标准化产品形态——公司与上市时间、适用人群定位（面向次标体还是健康体）、健康告知（健告类型、问项原文、疾病问询范围、住院/检查问项与时限、可正常投保情形、告知效力）、核保准入、保障责任、续保机制、既往症与赔付规则、费率，每个取值附原文引文、页码与来源并自动核验；做多产品与新旧版本对比，结合市场同类样本识别真正的创新点，输出 Excel 对比表，并可按需生成分析报告（Word）与汇报 PPT。Use for Chinese health/medical insurance (百万医疗、中高端医疗、长期医疗、免健告/简易健告/次标体) product research, clause & rate-table archiving, version comparison, innovation analysis. ([xuyue9129-ctrl/medical-insurance-product-research](https://github.com/xuyue9129-ctrl/medical-insurance-product-research/tree/HEAD/medical-insurance-product-research))

- **medical-literature-retrieval-workflow**: 从医学 EDA 报告自动提取研究要素、生成 PICO/PECO、构建 PubMed 检索式、检索近 3 年原始研究/近 5 年综述/不限时间精确组合文献、结构化摘要文献、构建 evidence gap matrix，并输出按创新性、数据可验证性、临床意义、统计可行性、发表潜力和风险评分的候选研究 idea。用于用户提供 EDA 报告、变量分析结果、临床数据探索报告、相关性分析结果后，希望寻找医学文献证据、识别研究空白、生成选题 idea 或完成从数据到论文选题的工作流。必须只使用真实可验证文献，不能编造题名、作者、期刊、年份、DOI、PMID、摘要、样本量或研究发现。 ([Tomo-Efficient/RWD-Research-Agent](https://github.com/Tomo-Efficient/RWD-Research-Agent/tree/HEAD/skills/medical-literature-retrieval-workflow) · ★1)
- **medical-literature-review**: Search and synthesize biomedical literature for medical, clinical, epidemiologic, public-health, and translational research questions. Use when the user asks what the literature says, asks for evidence on a medical claim, wants PubMed or biomedical database searching, asks for a literature review, or needs studies found, screened, compared, or summarized. Do not use for patient-specific diagnosis or treatment advice. ([yaacovcorcos/papiskill](https://github.com/yaacovcorcos/papiskill/tree/HEAD/registry/official/medical-literature-review) · ★2)
- **medical-literature-review**: 撰寫醫學文獻回顧（literature review）時使用。執行「三層外部知識管線 + 第二輪 PDF 深挖」流程：外部搜尋 → 全文落地 → 程式化比對，並在第一輪 PDF 閱讀完後追加第二輪參考文獻搜尋與下載。LLM 僅做改寫，所有事實必須能被 grep 驗回外部來源。觸發詞：「文獻回顧」「literature review」「整理 paper」「寫 review article」「醫學寫作」「PubMed / Europe PMC / OpenEvidence / Consensus 整合」。 ([zinojeng/medical-literature-review](https://github.com/zinojeng/medical-literature-review/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **medical-literature-review**: Systematically identify, evaluate, and synthesize published medical research to support evidence-based clinical practice and research planning. - **meditation**: A user-invoked pause, not a task. An extra beat inserted into the agent's Act→Observe→Act loop, turning it into Act→Observe₁→Observe₂→Act. Observe₂ is a repetition activity, not a list-making one — name what's arising in a short phrase, repeat the phrase a few times, let another observation surface, repeat that one too.
